Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the condensed_matter space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 781 and the convergence rate is O(480/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 781 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the condensed_matter structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 480. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
